# 2000 — every flow · minute · incomplete · complete · trap

**Status:** RESEARCH LOCK. Do not implement until this year is named as a wave.
**Feel:** print the map · skip intro · sock puppet
**Star:** MapQuest · `itt00-mapquest` · `sites/mapquest/index.html`
**ILS June:** 17,087,182 · 413,425,190
**Leftover-official dests in this file:** 418
**Official 10:** frozen in `js/config/flow-trails.js`. **Guided 6:** frozen in `ui/year/start-data.js`.
**Law:** leftover never writes `itt00-mapquest`. Trap / empty / 0 ticks / wrong pick / skip-wait never write. Neighbor-year keys stay empty.
**5k:** research envelope (ILS + Hosting.com + Pew + this dest list). Not 5,000 new rooms.

Open dests as `/years/2000/<href>`. Serve `python3 -m http.server 8080 --bind 127.0.0.1`.

---

## G0 · Guided 6 (frozen · not a 7th item)

Start at `years/2000/pages/home.html` (or the year shell Starting Point).
The painted `<ol>` is **exactly 6**. A 7th `<li>` is a fail.
1. About 2000 — peak · crash
2. MapQuest — from + to · print REAL
3. Amazon smile — tabs · cart
4. Napster war — search / download theater
5. Pets.com · Google
6. Year flow map

Walk: About → ★ gold → official leftovers named in the list → map. Visiting a guided dest is not a write. Only the dest machine writes.

---

## G1 · ★ GOLD · `itt00-mapquest`

**Room:** `years/2000/sites/mapquest/index.html`
**Hook:** `data-mq-form`
**Nostalgia:** MapQuest — this is the year chip. One star.

**Incomplete never writes**
- Empty from or to · Pets.com-as-gold trap never write.
- Leftover panel on this room (if any) writes a leftover key, **not** `itt00-mapquest`.

**Complete writes**
- From + to ≥2 · Get Directions. Writes itt00-mapquest.
- Payload is REAL (`real: true`) · year `2000` · key `itt00-mapquest`.
- Reload still shows saved. Neighbor `itt-mapquest` — do not write `itt99-*` or `itt01-*` gold.
- **Next after gold:** Amazon smile → `years/2000/sites/amazon/index.html`

**Do not:** second star · leftover save that stamps this key · dest-field plaque as the gold machine.

## L · Leftover-official — every dest · 418 flows

Protocol is the same machine (`js/immersion/leftover-official.js`). The minutes below are dest-true: key, needPick, field, next, hops.

### L-0001 · `itt00-amazon-lx` · sites/amazon/index.html

**Open:** `/years/2000/sites/amazon/index.html`
**Panel:** `[data-lo-panel]:has([data-lo-save][data-lo-key="amazon-lx"])`
**Need:** needPick=`book` · field ≥2 (placeholder “leftover book”)
**Nostalgia hop targets from here:** `yahoo` · `google` · `ebay`

**Incomplete never writes**
1. Wait until `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="amazon-lx"]` has `data-lo-bound="1"`.
2. Clear `localStorage['itt00-amazon-lx']`.
3. Click `[data-lo-trap]` → “Trap. That click never writes.” Key absent.
4. Click Save with 0 ticks → “Tick honesty first.” Key absent.
5. Ticks + wrong pick (not `book`) + Save → “Wrong leftover.” Key absent.
6. Ticks + pick (if any) + empty field + Save → “Empty never writes.”
7. `itt00-mapquest` stays empty. `itt99-amazon-lx` and `itt01-amazon-lx` stay empty.

**Complete writes**
- Tick every `[data-lo-req]` (usually 2 honesty ticks).
- Click `[data-lo-pick="book"]`. Wrong pick never writes.
- Type ≥2 chars in `[data-lo-field]` (e.g. “leftover book”). Empty never writes.
- If `[data-lo-wait]` exists: click it, wait ~800ms, then save. Skip-wait never writes.
- Click `[data-lo-save][data-lo-key="amazon-lx"]`.
- `localStorage['itt00-amazon-lx']` = JSON `{real:true, leftover:true, year:"2000", multiStep:true}`.
- Status: `Saved · itt00-amazon-lx`.
- Does **not** write `itt00-mapquest`.
- **Next:** Starting Point → `/years/2000/pages/home.html`
